Title: Viaggio del P. Dionigi de' Carli da Piacenza, e del P. Michel Angelo de' Guattini da Reggio, Capuccini, predicatori, e missionarij apostolici nel regno del Congo
Creator: Dionigi de' Carli da Piacenza and Michelangelo Guattini da Reggio, Capuchins; issued at the instance of Giovanni Guattini
Publisher: Reggio: per Prospero Vedrotti, 1671 (printer and year from the holding library's record; the imprint line is not cleanly legible in the scan)
Publication Date: 1671
Rights: Public Domain (published 1671, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item's own rights statement reads 'http://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/mark/1.0/')
Language: ita
Source: Internet Archive item bub_gb_CEgQlW96tsMC (https://archive.org/details/bub_gb_CEgQlW96tsMC), the item's own bub_gb_CEgQlW96tsMC_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: National Library of Naples; host metadata date: 1671. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: f3d31850433bce365dfb2a88311d097df3e87c94605bc6e702d119f989925be1.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. National Library of Naples scan via Google Books; clean OCR for its age, with the long s read as f. A small duodecimo; no Google notice in the text file.
Content note: The joint narrative of Guattini and Carli from the Capuchin mission of 1666 to 1670, Carli writing the later part alone: the voyage, the two friars' mission in the duchy of Bamba, and Carli's account of baptising and preaching in Kongo under King Alvaro.
